## The set of a confirmation

**R52. A judgement's confirmation runs only on its own held-out set. The table judgement → set lies in `internal/seed/sets.go`, beside the seeds: `model-2` — `held-out-model-1`, `engine-2` — `held-out-engine-1`, `engine-lesson-1` — `held-out-engine-lesson-1`. The judgement file names its judgement, as its folder, by `"judgement"`. `sim judge` refuses a confirmation without it, a set kept for another round or variant, and one another judgement has looked at. Д8 is closed.** The author's answer 2026-11-14, in the planning of T57, by the recommendation (a) (RUN.md, T57).
- **What was found in planning:**
  - `sim judge` held a confirmation only to the prefix `held-out-<criterion>-` and to a set apart from the choice's, so `engine-lesson-1` would have taken `held-out-engine-1`, kept for `engine-2`, and `model-3` would have taken `held-out-model-1`, which `model-2` looked at (R46; Д8);
  - the judgement file did not name its judgement: the folder `judgements/<name>/` is only where it lies, and no test read `judgements/`;
  - a confirmation's candidate on another set than its base is refused already, by the pairing of `bench.Cells`.
- **What is built:**
  - `seed.Confirmation(judgement)` gives the set of a judgement's row, `seed.Judgement(experiment)` the judgement whose set it is; the rounds that ended in an exit without a confirmation, `engine-1` and `model-1`, have no row;
  - `criterion.Input.Judgement`: a named judgement opens with its criterion; a confirmation needs it, and runs on its row's set and apart from the choice's; a set of another row names its judgement in the refusal; the report names the set of the confirmation;
  - the field `"judgement"` of the judgement file; without a confirmation it may stay out, so the files of `judgements/` and of rough looks decode as before. A file in a folder `judgements/<name>/` that names another judgement — a copy's leftover — is refused. On the present tree `sim judge` refuses the file of `model-2`, which has no `"judgement"`, as it already refused it by its base (R40, R46); on its own commit it gives its report again;
  - the tests: `held-out-engine-1` refused in `engine-lesson-1` and `held-out-model-1` in `model-3`, the own set taken; every recorded held-out set in exactly one row, of its judgement's criterion; the files of `judgements/` read by the decoder of `sim judge`, and their confirmations, by `runs.txt`, on their judgements' sets — `model-2` on `held-out-model-1`.

— *Why:* a round takes the first set of its criterion no confirmation has looked at (the 2026-11-10 amendment to R35), and a variant a set of its own (R49), so which set is a judgement's depends on the history of the exits and on the author's decisions, not on a rule of names. A table records that history where the seeds are, and a test holds every seed to one judgement.

— *Rejected:*
- (b) the judgement file names the set, and `sim judge` refuses the sets looked at, by their list — it would take `held-out-engine-1` in `engine-lesson-1`: nobody has looked at it, it is only kept for `engine-2`. To refuse it the list must know whose each set is, which is the table;
- (c) the set derived from the folder, with `engine-2` an exception — `model-2` confirmed on `held-out-model-1` and `model-3` goes on `held-out-model-2`, so the exceptions grow with every exit;
- the judgement's name from the folder of the file — a judgement file may lie anywhere, and the name is the file's to say;
- editing the files of `judgements/` — they are records, and on their own commits they give their reports again.

— *Consequence:* the 2026-11-14 amendments to R04, R25 and R35; Д8 closed. The merge releases a tag of `sim`, the pin of the judgement `engine-lesson-1` until its choice (RUN.md, epic E7). Where it is written: RUN.md, T57 ([docs/plan/E7.md](plan/E7.md)); `internal/seed/sets.go`, `internal/criterion/`, `main.go`, the tests; README, "Judging a candidate"; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3, 3.6 and 3.8; DEBT.md; `.claude/rules/gate.md`.

— *Amendment 2026-11-16 (T70; R65):* the row `model-fair-1` — `held-out-model-fair-1` comes into the table with T71, the code of R65, not with the 2026-11-16 amendment to R04 that records its seed: T70 changes only documents, and no run of the judgement goes before the tag of T71, which brings the row. Until then the set is recorded and no judgement takes it.

— *Amendment 2026-11-16 (T71; R66):* the row `model-fair-1` — `held-out-model-fair-1` is in the table, with its seed 20261116.

## The judgement `engine-lesson-1`

**R53. The judgement of the variant of consolidation in a lesson — T52 of `engine`, the commit 0e4680f of the branch `e7-consolidation-in-lesson` — ended in an exit: the candidate fails one constraint of the engine's criterion and is not better than the base. The base stays as it is. There was no confirmation, and `held-out-engine-lesson-1` is still held out. `gate.json`, `go.mod`, the paths and the bands do not change.** The judgement of T58 (RUN.md), by the criterion of R49 on the step of R48, on the base after `model-2` — `learner-model` v0.1.8 and `engine` v0.1.6 (R46); on `sim` at the tag `v0.1.17`, one pin for the whole judgement (RUN.md, epic E7). The report — `judgements/engine-lesson-1/` (R35).
- **The candidate** — `E7`, `0e4680f0631e113bf91f54846a0a4b1322c76219`, the head of the branch, which has no pull request and no run of CI (sources 4.8). Group `corridor`, no new constant, no new field, `distance` 1, `equal_on` `novatrail/grade-3-math` and `novatrail/english-essays`: the line of the judgement file came from «Открыто» of T52 `engine`. Its "Gate" was run by the session of `engine` on `v0.1.15`, locally, after its review: red only by two bands of zero width, `E2.stuck` on `G2-half` of olympiad maths and on `A-linked`, the invariants intact (sources 4.8). T58 ran it again on 0e4680f itself, on `v0.1.17`, `GATE_ENGINE=0e4680f… just gate`: the same two bands, 0.016 and 0.008, and no invariant among the reasons. So the repeat of the gate held `I1` to the bit on the commit judged, on which R33 rests.
- **The runs** — decisive, 2,000 working learners a cell, by the recipe `just decisive` (R34):
  - the base — `runs/base-t55.jsonl`, SHA-256 `2e53e18c…9aad`, the one that `paths.json` names;
  - `E7` — SHA-256 `b5c2a736…a38d`, 43 min 50 s by the clock, on a machine free at the start that neighbours loaded for a quarter of an hour (measurements 1.3).

  One twentieth of the run of `E7` was run anew and matched its file line for line, 6,500 lines.
- **The report** of `sim judge`:

  | candidate | score | group `corridor` | failed "no worse" |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | `E7` | −0.0018 [−0.0047; 0.0010] | −0.0002 [−0.0010; 0.0006] | 1 |

  - It fails one check, `M3.lag` of `G2-half` on olympiad maths: worse by at most 0.01812, at a tolerance of 0.01785. `I1`–`I4`, `E2` and `neutral` hold: on school maths and on the essays every learner's sums are the base's to the bit.
  - The note `E6`: on olympiad maths and the pair of maths the variant consolidates 0.76–1.01 times a pass; the items of a consolidation are in the corridor 0.27–0.43 of the time, about twice as often as those of the lesson's segments, but on almost every cell the segments themselves go up to 1.4 points less often into the corridor and more often into "hard", and `E6.next`, P* of the next segment after a failure, is lower by up to 0.023. What a consolidation gains in the corridor the lesson loses.
  - The rough look of `engine` on 300 learners also saw no gain, and failed other cells, `E2.stuck` on `G2-half` and `E4.given` on `G5` and `S-scale3.5` (sources 4.8): at 2,000 learners these hold, and `M3.lag` on `G2-half` does not.

— *Why an exit:* the choice takes only a candidate that holds all the constraints and is better than the base ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.6). `E7` is neither: its group is about zero and its score below zero, both within the noise, and it fails `M3.lag` by a hair. That it fails by a hair is no argument to shift the tolerance, which was written down before the first candidate (R04, R49). The lag of the model on `G2` is the common failure of A and C in `engine-1` too (R36): a new order of tasks within the corridor gives the model less evidence where the truth is learning. The note says why the corridor does not grow: the consolidations take the corridor's items out of the segments that follow them. This is a signal for `engine`, not a verdict on consolidation as such: the simulator's truth knows no separate benefit of an answer right after an error (R49).

— *Rejected:*
- a confirmation — it runs only for a chosen candidate (section 3.6), and without it `held-out-engine-lesson-1` stays held out;
- a rough look of `sim` before the decisive run — the machine was free, and the look decides nothing (R49).

— *Consequence:*
- the base stays `learner-model` v0.1.8 and `engine` v0.1.6; the merge releases no tag, since neither `go.mod` nor `gate.json` changes (R29). T59, the move of the base to the release of the chosen one, has nothing to move: its status is the author's, `[-]`;
- T53 of `engine` records the exit by the link to `judgements/engine-lesson-1/` on `main`; T51 of `engine` does not change `spec`'s contract or cards, which change only if a judgement chooses the variant. The outcome goes to `spec` as row В19 «в реестр spec» of table 3 of RUN.md;
- `held-out-engine-lesson-1` is untouched and stays the set of `engine-lesson-1` in `internal/seed/sets.go`: a set is one judgement's (R52). A next judgement of the variant, if `engine` makes one, gets its folder, its own set and its row by an amendment to R04 before its first run;
- the round of the corridor, `engine-2` of epic E6, and the round of the model, `model-3` of epic E8, go on the same base, which neither had to wait for: for `model-3` the base and the tag of its exploration stay what they are (row В16 of table 3 of RUN.md).

Where it is written: RUN.md, T58 ([docs/plan/E7.md](plan/E7.md)), and table 3, В19; `judgements/engine-lesson-1/`; [architecture.md](architecture.md), the status and sections 3 and 3.6; [measurements.md](measurements.md), 1.3; [sources.md](sources.md), 4.8.

## Epic E9

**R54. Epic E9 is «Справедливость модели по классу», laid out before E8. Its tasks are T61–T65: the criterion of the fairness of the learner model by the grade, written down before its first run; the populations and the measure; the constraint in the model's criterion; the evaluation of the base for dpia 10.2 spec; and the summing up. The criterion takes option (b) of subsection 2.6 of architecture: it is an evaluation of the base and also a constraint of the model's criterion, "no worse than the base", in force before the first candidate of `model-3`, so E8 waits for its tag. No task moves `sim` to `spec`: no tag has T149.** The author's answers 2026-11-14: «разворачивай эпик E9 в задачи», by the proposal of T60, and the place (b), by the recommendation of subsection 2.6. The rest is a proposal of the planning.
- **What was found** (2026-11-14; sources 3.9 and 7.7, read again that day):
  - `spec` v0.17.5 is the latest tag, and `main` is on it. T149 is not done, so no tag has the key `entries` of Д9, and the first task that table 2 named for E9, the move to a tag with T149, has no tag to take;
  - T52 of `learner-model`, the exploration of `model-3`, has not begun. E8 has no candidates, and E9 waits for none;
  - every stream of a learner is keyed by the set, the experiment, the unit and the purpose. A new population leaves the lines of the 65 cells as they are, but it changes a decisive run's file that holds it: its header, its lines and its SHA-256;
  - `learner.Assumptions` can neither fix the grade nor give learners of different grades one truth;
  - `run.Obs` holds no band of the issued task, while the evidence of a step does;
  - the check "no worse" compares one cell of the base with the same cell of a candidate, while fairness compares the cells of the grades with one another. So its constraint is a check of its own in `sim judge`.
- **The tasks:**
  - T61 — the criterion: the populations, the measure, the numbers and the constraint, by a decision and an amendment to R04, before any run of the new populations on the published packages;
  - T62 — the populations and the measure in code, with the bits of the 65 cells the same;
  - T63 — the constraint in `sim judge`; its merge releases the tag that the judgement `model-3` pins;
  - T64 — the evaluation of the base by a decisive run, its result recorded and sent «в реестр spec» for dpia 10.2 spec;
  - T65 — the summing up.

  The order and what is common to the tasks are in RUN.md, table 1 and «Эпик E9».

— *Why:*
- **E9 now.** Only E9 can start. R50 rejected it as the first epic while both rounds were under way and both libraries waited for a judgement of `sim`. Now the variant's round has ended (R53), and `learner-model` waits for its own exploration. Row 4.16 has a deadline: the first child from the US (R147 spec).
- **The place (b).** If a chosen candidate moved the base, dpia 10.2 spec would describe a model that no longer runs. A constraint written down before the first candidate of `model-3` keeps any chosen candidate no less fair than the evaluated base. Every constraint of R04 is likewise written before its round's first candidate.
- **The criterion apart from the code.** The author reads the criterion line by line before any code runs it, and its numbers come before the first run (R147 spec; R163 standalone).
- **The bits of the 65 cells.** `learner-model` explores on `v0.1.14`, and its numbers stand beside a judgement of `sim` only while those lines stay the same (R32 learner-model; row В16 of RUN.md). T62 checks this as T56 did (R51).

— *Rejected:*
- E8 first — it has no candidates;
- the place (a), an evaluation alone, repeated when the base moves — a round would choose without seeing fairness, and the loss would show only after the release;
- a move as the first task — there is no tag with T149. A tag of `spec` with the same bits that comes out during E9 is taken by a task of its own, by R45;
- the criterion and its code in one task, as T55 did for consolidation in a lesson (R49) — the author would read the numbers beside the code that already runs them.

— *Consequence:* the 2026-11-14 amendment to R50. The row E9 left table 2 of RUN.md, and E8 waits for the tag of T63; rows В16 and В18 of table 3 say so. Where it is written: RUN.md, tables 1–3 and «Эпик E9»; [architecture.md](architecture.md), the status, subsection 2.6 and "What stays open"; [sources.md](sources.md), 3.9 and 7.7; README.

— *Amendment 2026-11-14 (T61; R55):* the constraint of fairness holds the engine's criterion and a pair too, not only the model's: the kind of task is the engine's choice, and a chosen candidate of `engine-2` would leave dpia 10.2 spec stale just as one of `model-3` would. So E6 waits for the tag of T63 as E8 does.

— *Amendment 2026-11-14 (T64; R58), the author's answer in the planning of T64:* E8 and E6 wait for the tag of T64, not of T63: the constraint `fair` is T63's and the same on both tags, but the merge of T64 changes `paths.json`, which names the base's file of 68 cells, and only on its tag does `just decisive engine` give that file (the 2026-11-14 amendment to R57).

— *Amendment 2026-11-15 (R59):* E8 and E6 no longer wait in table 2 of RUN.md: they left it, and a judgement comes only with a library's candidate that has a reason of its own. If `model-3` or `engine-2` comes so, it pins the tag of T64 or a later one, as above.

## The fairness by the grade

**R55. The criterion of the fairness of the learner model by the grade, written down before its first run. The populations `D-grade1`, `D-grade3` and `D-grade5` on olympiad maths differ by the grade alone, with one truth and one unit for the learner n of all three. The measure `M11` reads, against the reference grade 3, the number of answers until the module level holds within 0.5 for ten answers in a row and the bands of the tasks after the 50th answer. The base holds when grades 1 and 5 both come to the same estimate by the 50th answer for at least 0.90 of the learners and their bands differ by at most 5 points. The constraint `fair` — no worse than the base — holds the criteria of the model and of the engine and a pair. The cells are in the decisive run, and the report of an evaluation lies in `evaluations/fairness-<n>/`.** The author's answers 2026-11-14, in the planning of T61, all by the recommendations (RUN.md, T61); the place of the criterion is R54, its request R147 spec and row 4.16 of `spec`'s COORDINATION.md.
- **The populations.** `D-grade1`, `D-grade3` and `D-grade5`, on `novatrail/olympiad-math` alone: only it names more than one grade, three bands for grades 1–2, 3–4 and 5–6 ([architecture.md](architecture.md), subsection 2.6). Each holds the assumptions of `G0` — the miss 0.5, no learning — with the grade fixed at 1, 3 or 5, one a band, so the model starts on bands 1, 2 and 3; two grades of one band give the model the same start. The reference is `D-grade3`. The truth of the learner n is the same in all three, the one grade 3 gives: θ* = 2.5 · (2 − 1) + N(0, 1) and each δ* from N(0, 0.5²). All six places of the learner n — `params`, `calendar`, `answers`, `retries`, `attempts` and `attempt` — are those of one unit, the reference's `D-grade3/<n>`, so the learner n of each grade has the same days, the same numbers for its answers in the same order and the same seeds of attempts. The age category is `under_13`, so the pool and the lessons are the same as well. The only difference is the grade, and with it the model's start: 2.5 below the truth, at it and 2.5 above it.
- **The measure `M11`**, fairness by the grade, is read on `D-grade1` and `D-grade5`, the learner n against the learner n of `D-grade3`, on the module level θ the model gives after each answer — the k-th first answer of an item with a verdict, as `M3` counts:
  - `M11.answers` — the number of answers k from which |θ of the grade − θ of the reference| holds below 0.5 for ten answers in a row, counted as `M3` counts catching up: the first answer of the series. A learner for whom it never holds within the run counts as 200, the run's length, so the mean is over all the learners, and a candidate that leaves a slow learner never coming to the same estimate is worse, not better;
  - `M11.same` — the share of learners whose k is at most N = 50. Fifty answers are five sessions, a week of the calendar. On olympiad maths an attempt is almost always one item — `G0` makes 184 choices for 200 answers — so the answers are about the attempts R147 spec names;
  - `M11.band` — on the attempts with a verdict whose first answer is the 51st or later, to the end of the run, the tasks of lessons and consolidations among them: for each of the three bands, the share of these attempts at that band, over all the learners of the cell. The number is the largest absolute difference, over the bands, between the grade's share and the reference's.

  The intervals are 95 %, from 2,000 resamples of the learners' numbers n, the same n in all three cells, so the difference is paired.
- **The evaluation.** The base holds the criterion when, for grade 1 and for grade 5 alike, `M11.same` is at least 0.90 at the lower end of its interval and `M11.band` at most 0.05 at the upper end. `M11.answers` is reported with its interval and without a bound. A base that does not hold is recorded as it is: R147 spec asks for the results, whatever they are, and these numbers are not changed after the first run (R163 standalone). An evaluation is made of the base and repeated whenever the base moves, by the task that moves it, as the paths are read anew.
- **The constraint `fair`** is a check of its own kind, beside `neutral`, in the model's criterion, in the engine's criterion and for a pair, at the choice and in the confirmation. On `D-grade1` and `D-grade5`, the shortfall of `M11.same` (1 − the share), `M11.answers` and `M11.band` are each "no worse" than the base: the worse end of the 95 % interval of the paired difference of the candidate and the base is within the tolerance. The tolerance is the larger of the author's — 0.01 for the two shares, one answer for `M11.answers` — and 4.3 standard errors of that difference, as for "no worse" in [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.6. Less is better for all three. `M11` is in no score group, has no path and no target, and the gate does not read it. A run without the cells leaves the constraint unmet: olympiad maths is published, so the exception "no graph" does not apply.
- **The place in the runs.** The three cells are in the decisive run, in the confirmation and in a rough look: 44 populations and 68 cells, 2,000 learners a cell. The engine's ceiling does not run them — `M11` has no path — and its file stays as it is. Nor does the gate, whose 69 numbers stay. The lines of the 65 cells stay byte for byte. The base's file changes by the three cells, so T64 runs it anew, and `paths.json` names its new SHA-256 with the same values, as for the note of T55 (R49). The time is measured on the existing cells of olympiad maths, without the new populations (measurements 1.3): about 0.14 CPU seconds a learner, so the three cells add about 14 CPU minutes to the 727 of the base of T55, 1.9 %. By the runs of T48 and T58 that is about 44–45 minutes by the clock, at the 45 of R38. If the base of T64 passes them on a free machine, the first cut is that of [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3, "What stays open": the input of the calls further only for the first 10 learners of a cell.
- **The report** of an evaluation lies in `evaluations/fairness-<n>/`, n from 1: the evaluation file with the SHA-256 of the run, and the report with the criterion in a paragraph citing this decision, the versions of the base, the tag of `sim`, the numbers of grades 1 and 5 with their intervals, whether the base holds, the date, and the paragraph for dpia 10.2 spec. It goes to `spec` as a row «в реестр spec».
- **The names.** `D-` stands for disparate impact: Colorado's Rule 9.06(F)(10) asks how a profiling system "is evaluated for fairness and disparate impact", as R147 spec quotes it, read 2026-11-13 (sources 3.12 spec). `M11` is the next measure of the model, and `fair` is the name of the constraint.

— *Why:*
- **One truth and one unit.** With one truth "the same skill" is exact, and with all the places shared the difference between the grades is the grade's, not the seeds'. So the comparison is paired learner by learner and needs no more learners than a cell has.
- **The truth on the middle band.** Grades 1 and 5 then start a band below and a band above it, so the criterion sees a learner ahead of their grade and one behind it.
- **No learning.** In `G2` the truth grows with the skills the engine gives, so under two grades it would part after the start, and "the same skill" would hold only at the first answer.
- **The module level.** The grade sets only the module level's start; the skills' offsets start at zero under any grade.
- **A number of answers with a bound.** R147 spec asks for a number of attempts. Ten answers in a row and 0.5 are the catching up of `M3`, which the model's criterion already holds. Fifty answers is a week of practice, enough for the trial series and the following sessions to correct one band.
- **The band.** It is the module's scale of hardness, and the start band moves it. Shares over the cell compare what the two grades are given, while the histories of single learners differ after the start.
- **Both criteria.** The engine chooses the task, and it moves the estimate too, by which tasks it gives. A chosen candidate of either library that left the base less fair would leave dpia 10.2 spec stale (R54).
- **The decisive run.** Every candidate and every confirmation runs the cells by itself, with the same recipe and one file. The time allows it.
- **A folder of its own.** An evaluation chooses nothing, and the test of R52 reads every file of `judgements/` as a judgement file.

— *Rejected:*
- only `params` shared — the calendar and the answers of each grade would add the noise of the seeds to the difference;
- the truth on band 1, with grade 1 as the reference — only a learner behind their grade, with grade 5 five logits above the truth;
- the truth by the grade, compared by the error θ − θ* (option (b) of subsection 2.6) — "the same skill" only on average;
- N = 100 — half of the run, too late to say that two grades come to one estimate;
- `M11.answers` as the mean over the learners who come, as `M3.settle` — a candidate would gain by leaving the slowest learners never to come;
- the difference after exactly N answers (option (a) of subsection 2.6) — one cut is noisier and gives no number of attempts;
- the bucket of `E1` as the kind of task — with one truth it nearly repeats the estimate, and it says how hard the task is for the learner, not what kind it is; the same task — the histories part after the start, and the number says little of fairness;
- a separate run with a size of its own — a second file for every candidate and every confirmation, and a recipe of its own;
- the constraint in the model's criterion alone — see "Why";
- `judgements/fairness-<n>/` — see "Why"; `Y-` for the populations — "year" names the grade in some countries only.

— *Consequence:* the 2026-11-14 amendments to R03, R04, R22 and R54; subsection 2.6 is decided. T62 builds the populations, `M11` and the report of an evaluation; T63 builds `fair` and releases the tag that `model-3` and `engine-2` pin; T64 evaluates the base. Where it is written: RUN.md, T61 ([docs/plan/E9.md](plan/E9.md)), table 1, «Эпик E9», tables 2 and 3; [architecture.md](architecture.md), the status, subsection 2.6, sections 3, 3.2, 3.4, 3.5, 3.6 and 3.8; [measurements.md](measurements.md), 1.3; README.

— *Amendment 2026-11-14 (T62; R56), the author's answers in the planning of T62, both by the recommendations:* the count k of `M11.answers` is that of `M3` (R22): the answers before the first answer of the series of ten, 0 when it holds from the first answer, so that `M11.same` is k at most 50 — the series begins by the 51st answer at the latest, as `M11.band` counts from the 51st —, and a learner for whom it never holds counts as 200. The words "from the 50th answer at the latest" of [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.5, meant this, and are corrected there. Rejected: k as the place of the first answer of the series. And the cells of the populations `D-` are read by the constraint `fair` alone: the checks "no worse" on every population, the floors, `neutral`, the score and the note `E6` stay on the 41 populations of the 65 cells, as they were written down before the first run (the amendment to R04 of that day). Rejected: every check on the cells `D-` too — three cells this decision did not weigh. The code is R56.

— *Amendment 2026-11-14 (T63; R57):* the constraint `fair` is in `sim judge`, the check kind `"fair"`, three checks on each of `D-grade1` and `D-grade5`, once in a pair; its pairs are resampled by the reference's cell, as in the evaluation. A candidate whose cells `D-` are not the base's — without them against a base with them, or the other way round — refuses the judgement, the cells of both named; runs that both lack them fail `fair`, each of its checks. The merge of T63 releases the tag that `model-3` and `engine-2` pin.

— *Amendment 2026-11-14 (T64; R58):* the base was evaluated, `evaluations/fairness-1/`, and does not hold the criterion: `M11.same` is 0.812 [0.795; 0.829] for grade 1 and 0.5525 [0.53; 0.5735] for grade 5, below 0.90, while `M11.band` is within 0.05 for both. The bounds stay as written; the result goes to dpia 10.2 spec as it is, and what follows is the author's (R58). The judgements `model-3` and `engine-2` pin the tag of T64, whose `paths.json` names the base's file of 68 cells (the 2026-11-14 amendment to R57).

— *Amendment 2026-11-16 (T70; R65):* `M11` is still in no score group and has no path, but `M11.same` is the target of the judgement `model-fair-1`: the mean of the paired differences of grades 1 and 5, its lower end above zero, and that target alone decides "better" there. The bounds of the evaluation are no condition of that judgement: its report says whether the chosen one holds them on its confirmation. `fair` holds there as it is.

## Fairness by the grade in code

**R56. The populations of fairness by the grade, `M11` and the evaluation in code: the assumptions `Grade` and `TruthGrade`; a population's `Reference`, whose units every population of the family takes; the trace of `M11` in the line of a learner of fairness, under the engine alone; the pairs of a grade and the reference read as a cell, resampled by the reference's cell; the engine's ceiling without the populations of fairness, and a judgement that reads their cells by `fair` alone; the command `sim evaluate` and the evaluation file in `evaluations/fairness-<n>/`.** Decided by the executor 2026-11-14 in T62 (RUN.md, T62; R55); the count of `M11.answers` and the cells read by `fair` alone are the author's answers in its planning (the 2026-11-14 amendment to R55).
- **The truth.** `learner.Assumptions.Grade` fixes the learner's grade — the profile's, by which the model starts — and `TruthGrade` the grade whose start band shifts the truth's module level. The grade is still drawn from `params`, and the value replaced, so the order of R15 holds. A module keeps the model's start band, `StartBand`, and the truth's, `TrueBand`, apart; without `TruthGrade` they are one. A grade its modules do not name in common, or a grade on modules with none, is refused. `D-grade1`, `D-grade3` and `D-grade5` are `G0` with `Grade` 1, 3 or 5 and `TruthGrade` 3, on olympiad maths: the list `population.Fairness`, after `Own`.
- **The unit.** `Population.Reference`, `D-grade3` for all three, names the population whose units its learners take, `Units()`: the unit is `seed.Learner(Units(), n)` for all six purposes, and the line keeps the population's own name. The test that no learner is drawn alike in two sets reads each unit once.
- **The trace.** `run.Obs.Band` is the place of the band of the attempt's task. `measure.Traced` gives θ of the model after each of the first 200 answers — each answer of an attempt has the level after the attempt, as `M3` counts — and, by band, the attempts with a verdict whose first answer is the 51st or later. The line of a learner of fairness carries it as `fair`, under the engine; no other line has the field, so the lines of the 65 cells are the bytes they were. The oracle has no `M11`: its level is the truth, one under every grade.
- **The pairs.** `bench.Fair` pairs the lines of each grade's cell with the reference's on the same graph, learner by learner; `measure.Fair` gives a pair's sums — `M11.answers.sum`, `M11.same`, the attempts of both at each band — and `measure.FairNumbers` reads `M11.answers`, `M11.same` and `M11.band` off them. A cell of pairs reads as a cell of learners, its resamples drawn by the name of the reference's cell, so that every grade and both sides of a judgement take the same n. The criterion's directions hold `M11`: `M11.answers` plain with one answer, `M11.same` by its shortfall and `M11.band` plain, each with 0.01; a judgement reads none of them off a cell of learners.
- **The ceiling and the judgement.** `bench.Populations` names the populations a rule runs, those of fairness left out under the ceiling, and `sim run -populations all` takes them; naming one under the ceiling is refused, so the ceiling's file is as it was. `bench.Cells` leaves their lines out of both sides against the ceiling, so `sim paths` reads a base of 68 cells against a ceiling of 65 to the paths of the 65, and each cell finds its oracle by its name, wherever the cells of fairness stand in the file. A judgement leaves their cells out of all it reads but `fair`, T63's — the checks "no worse", the floors, `neutral`, the score and the note `E6` —, and the invariants hold on every line.
- **The evaluation.** `sim evaluate <evaluation file>` reads the evaluation file — its name, as its folder `evaluations/<name>/`, the run relative to it, the run's SHA-256 in hex and the date, YYYY-MM-DD, a day that exists —, refuses a run of another SHA-256, and writes the report to stdout: the criterion, citing R55; the libraries, the version of `sim` from the build, the run's learners and SHA-256 and the date; each grade's `M11` with its intervals and the bounds it holds; whether the base holds; and the paragraph for dpia 10.2 spec. It reads only a whole decisive run of the base under the engine, on the working learners, the pinned packages and the libraries of the embedded gate file, with its invariants recorded and without a breach, and with each cell of fairness on a published graph. The evaluation file and the report, `report.md`, lie in `evaluations/fairness-<n>/`, written by hand and by `sim evaluate` as a judgement's are, and a test reads every folder there.

— *Why:*
- **Two fields, not one.** The grade the model starts by and the truth's are two assumptions; a fixed grade alone would move the truth with it, as in every other population.
- **The draw kept.** `params` in its order is what the test of R15 holds, and the truth of the three grades is one only if each draws the same numbers.
- **A trace in the line.** The pair needs both learners' levels answer by answer, and a line is one learner (R24); a file holds both cells, so the pairs are read off one file.
- **The reference's cell for the resamples.** It gives the same n in all three cells, as R55 asks, with no change to the resamples of `measure`.
- **`M11` apart from `ModelNumbers`.** It is read off pairs; among the numbers of a cell of learners `sim compare` would write it unread on every cell.
- **A date in the file.** The report is then the same bytes on every reading, and the code reads no clock.

— *Rejected:*
- the pairs' sums written by the run into the line — a line would hold another learner's numbers, and a run of one population could not give them;
- the trace on every line — the lines of the 65 cells would change, and with them every file of a decisive run;
- the unit through a field of `Assumptions` — the unit is the population's, not the learner's truth;
- `invariant.Same` extended to the trace — the trace follows from the evidence and the model's states, which `I1` holds, as every measure does (R17);
- the date from the clock.

— *Consequence:* T63 reads `fair` off `bench.Fair` of the base and of a candidate; T64 runs the base on 68 cells and writes `evaluations/fairness-1/`. Where it is written: RUN.md, T62; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3, 3.1, 3.2, 3.4, 3.5 and 3.6; README; `internal/learner/`, `internal/population/`, `internal/run/`, `internal/measure/`, `internal/bench/`, `internal/criterion/`, `main.go`.

## The constraint of fairness in code

**R57. The constraint `fair` in code: a check of its own kind, `"fair"`, which `sim judge` gives in the model's criterion, the engine's and a pair — once in a pair — at the choice and in the confirmation. On the cell of each grade but the reference's it reads the pairs of `bench.Fair` of the candidate against those of the base, learner by learner, as one cell of the reference's name, and holds each number of `M11` "no worse", by the same check as on any cell. A grade whose cell neither run has fails it; a candidate whose cells of fairness are not the base's, or a line of fairness without its trace, refuses the judgement, naming the side. `sim compare` does not write `M11`.** Decided by the executor 2026-11-14 in T63 (RUN.md, T63; R55, R56).
- **The check.** `fair` gives, for `D-grade1` and `D-grade5` on olympiad maths, three checks each: `M11.same` by its shortfall, `M11.answers` and `M11.band` as they are, by their directions in `defs`. The worse end of the 95 % interval of the paired difference, the candidate's less the base's, is within the larger of the author's tolerance — 0.01, one answer, 0.01 — and 4.3 standard errors of that difference: the code of "no worse" is one function, `notWorseCheck`, for both kinds. A number the base does not read has nothing to hold, as for "no worse"; one the candidate's run does not read is unmet.
- **The pairs.** The pairs of a grade and the reference of both sides are read by `measure.ReadPair` under the name of the reference's cell, `D-grade3/novatrail/olympiad-math`, so that both sides and both grades resample the same n, and the base's numbers are those `sim evaluate` reads off the same file (R56). The base's pairs are read once a judgement, and those of the confirmation's base once, so an error in them names the base. Before the files are paired, the cells of fairness of a candidate are held to the base's: a candidate without the cells `D-` against a base with them, or the other way round, is refused, the cells of both named, not with the count of learners that a run short of a population gets. The pairing of the whole files then holds the learners of the two sides to one another.
- **A run without the cells.** When neither run has the cell of a grade, each of its three checks fails, with the cell and the reference named: olympiad maths is published, so the exception "no graph" does not apply (R55).
- **The report.** A failed check is written as any other, `fails fair D-grade1/novatrail/olympiad-math M11.band: worse by at most …, the tolerance …`: the population, the graph and the number where the constraint does not hold. The walk over the grades and their cells is one, `grades`, for `fair` and the evaluation.

— *Why:*
- **One function for "no worse".** R55 writes `fair` as "no worse" on other cells; one function keeps the tolerance and the reading of the interval the same.
- **Once in a pair.** A pair holds the constraints of both criteria once each (R25), and `fair` is the same constraint in both.
- **The reference's name for the resamples.** It is what R56 chose for the evaluation, and it makes the judgement's numbers of the base those of the evaluation.
- **`M11` not in `sim compare`.** `sim compare` writes the numbers of the cells of learners; `M11` is read off pairs of two cells, and its readers are the judgement, by `fair`, and the evaluation.

— *Rejected:*
- `fair` only in the criterion the candidate names, not in a pair's second — R55 holds both criteria and a pair;
- a run without the cells as "no graph" — R55: it leaves `fair` unmet;
- `M11` in `sim compare` beside the numbers of the cells — a second reading of pairs, which neither the choice nor the evaluation needs;
- a refusal of cells of fairness on a graph outside their population's, as `sim evaluate` refuses one with no published package — `sim run` gives the cells of a population only on its graphs, olympiad maths for `D-`, and the check names the cell it does not find.

— *Consequence:* the 2026-11-14 amendments to R25 and R55. The merge releases a tag of `sim` (R29), the pin of the judgements `model-3` and `engine-2` (R54, R55). Where it is written: RUN.md, T63; [architecture.md](architecture.md), the status, sections 3 and 3.6; README, "Judging a candidate"; `internal/criterion/`.

— *Amendment 2026-11-14 (T63):* the tag is `v0.1.19`, released on the merge commit 7553035 of pull request #69 and accepted by `gh release verify`; rows В16 and В20 of table 3 of RUN.md name it.

— *Amendment 2026-11-14 (T64; R58), the author's answer in the planning of T64:* the judgements `model-3` and `engine-2` pin the tag of T64, not `v0.1.19`. The merge of T64 changes `paths.json`, which names the base's file of 68 cells, and so releases a tag (R29): on it `just decisive engine` gives the file that `paths.json` names, as step 1 of "Judging a candidate" in README says, while on `v0.1.19` it gives a file that `paths.json` there does not name. The code, the bits and `fair` of the two tags are the same. Rejected: `v0.1.19` as the pin — for the reason above. Rows В16 and В20 of table 3 of RUN.md say so.

— *Amendment 2026-11-15 (T64):* the tag of T64 is `v0.1.20`, released on the merge commit 63288d7 of pull request #71 and accepted by `gh release verify`; `model-3` and `engine-2` pin it, and rows В16 and В20 of table 3 of RUN.md name it.

## The evaluation `fairness-1`

**R58. The base — `learner-model` v0.1.8 and `engine` v0.1.6 — does not hold the criterion of fairness by the grade of R55. In the evaluation `evaluations/fairness-1/`, on `sim` v0.1.19, grades 1 and 5 come to the estimate of grade 3 by the 50th answer for 0.812 [0.795; 0.829] and 0.5525 [0.53; 0.5735] of the learners, below the bound 0.90; the bands of their tasks after the 50th answer differ from grade 3's by at most 0.0067 [0.0041; 0.0102] and 0.0127 [0.0073; 0.0180], within the bound 0.05. The result is recorded as it is and goes to dpia 10.2 spec; the criterion does not change. The base's file of 68 cells is the base of `model-3` and `engine-2`, and `paths.json` names it.** The executor's record 2026-11-14 in T64, by R55 (RUN.md, T64); the pin is the author's answer in its planning (the 2026-11-14 amendment to R57); what follows from the result is the author's, row В22 of table 3 of RUN.md.
- **The run.** `just decisive engine runs/base-t64.jsonl` from a clean clone of `sim` at the tag `v0.1.19`, by `go.mod`: 44 populations, 68 cells, 2,000 working learners a cell, 136,000 lines, SHA-256 `9e89cb7cfd0f1f0c00bb69c057a273aff0071e11b97450859d7b268e48c07763`. Past its header and without the 6,000 lines of the populations `D-`, the file is byte for byte `runs/base-t55.jsonl` (`cmp`), and the header differs only by the three populations, so the bits of the 65 cells are those of T55. Part 7 of 20 run anew by the same build, 6,800 lines, matched the file line for line. It took 43 min 28 s by the clock on a machine free at its start (measurements 1.3).
- **The numbers**, off `sim evaluate`, which gives `report.md` again to the byte:

  | Grade | `M11.answers` | `M11.same` | `M11.band` | Holds |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | 1 | 32.52 [30.62; 34.36] | 0.812 [0.795; 0.829] | 0.0067 [0.0041; 0.0102] | no: `M11.same` |
  | 5 | 83.33 [79.72; 87.23] | 0.5525 [0.53; 0.5735] | 0.0127 [0.0073; 0.0180] | no: `M11.same` |

  The intervals are 95 %, the learners paired by n with grade 3. The kind of task holds: after the 50th answer the shares of the bands of both grades differ from grade 3's by at most 1.3 points, 1.8 at the upper end of the interval. The estimate does not: one learner in five under grade 1 and nearly one in two under grade 5 is not yet within 0.5 of the estimate under grade 3 for ten answers in a row by the 50th answer. Grade 5, whose model starts 2.5 above the truth, comes to it later than grade 1, which starts 2.5 below it: 83 answers against 33 on average, a learner who never comes counting as 200. Why the start above the truth is left more slowly is not measured here; it is a question for `learner-model`.
- **The paths.** `sim paths` on this file and the ceiling of T48, `00ad8bb7…`, gives the table of measurements 3.2 to the last digit; `paths.json` changes only the base's SHA-256 (the 2026-11-14 amendment to R28). Its merge releases the tag that `model-3` and `engine-2` pin.
- **What it means for a candidate.** `fair` holds a candidate "no worse" than this base, so a candidate may not make grades 1 and 5 slower or their bands further apart; it is not rewarded for making them faster, since `M11` is in no score group (R55). A round whose aim is to bring the base within the bounds needs a target of its own, written down before its first candidate.

— *Why:*
- **As it is.** R147 spec asks for the results of the evaluation, whatever they are, and R55 wrote the bounds down before the first run so that they are not moved after it (R163 standalone).
- **A clone at the tag.** The report names the version of `sim` from the build; a build at the tag gives `v0.1.19`, while one on `main` after it gives a pseudo-version, and one with the task's edits a version marked dirty. The run's file names no version of `sim`, and the code of `main` was that of the tag.
- **A reading, not a cause.** The evaluation measures; why the model leaves a high start more slowly is `learner-model`'s to find, with its own measurements.

— *Rejected:*
- bounds read anew after the run — against R55 and R163 standalone;
- holding the result back until a fairer candidate — dpia 10.2 spec would go on saying that no evaluation was made;
- the run and the report built on `main` with the task's edits — the report would name no tag.

— *Consequence:* the 2026-11-14 amendments to R28, R54, R55 and R57. Row В21 of table 3 of RUN.md carries the result «в реестр spec» for item 10 of dpia 10.2 spec and row 4.16 of `spec`'s COORDINATION.md, and row В22 asks what follows. Where it is written: RUN.md, T64; [architecture.md](architecture.md), the status, subsection 2.6, sections 3 and 3.6; [measurements.md](measurements.md), 1.3 and 3.2; README; `evaluations/fairness-1/`; `internal/criterion/paths.json`; `.claude/rules/gate.md`.

— *Amendment 2026-11-15 (T64):* the tag of T64 is `v0.1.20`, released on the merge commit 63288d7 of pull request #71 and accepted by `gh release verify`; the 2026-11-14 amendment to R57 and rows В16, В20 and В21 of table 3 of RUN.md name it.

## Judgements on request

**R59. `sim` holds no rounds by plan. It stays the gate in the CI of `learner-model` and `engine`, and a judgement is laid out as an epic only when a library brings a candidate with a reason of its own — a fault that the gate or a measurement found, a need of the product, a row of `spec` — and names it. The epics E8, `model-3`, and E6, `engine-2`, leave table 2 of RUN.md. The one judgement in the queue is that of a round of `learner-model` aimed at the fairness by the grade, option (a) of row В22: `sim` writes down its criterion before its first candidate, once `learner-model` takes the round. An epic of `sim` has no separate task of summing up: its last task archives it and proposes what follows.** The author's answer 2026-11-15, after T65: «да согласен», to the session's proposal on the question «что за бесконечные круги мы делаем? зачем и какой результат?».
- **What was found** (2026-11-15):
  - of four judgements one chose — `model-2`, `theta` (R46); `engine-1`, `model-1` and `engine-lesson-1` ended in an exit (R36, R37, R53). The evaluation `fairness-1` found that the base does not hold the criterion of fairness by the grade (R58);
  - the candidates of a round seek small gains on virtual learners whose speeds and laws of forgetting are assumptions, not checked against live learners (Д3), and the criterion holds a candidate "no worse" on every cell, so most rounds end in an exit;
  - epics E5, E7 and E9 took $230.34 of tokens (measurements 2.2), and a share of it went to summing up, records for `spec` and archives rather than to judgements;
  - `engine` has three candidates of its epic E5, D, E and F, ready for `engine-2` (sources 4.9); `learner-model` has not begun the exploration of `model-3` (sources 3.9).

— *Why:*
- **The gate is the lasting value.** It holds every change of both libraries to the bands in their CI (section 3.9 of [architecture.md](architecture.md)), and it needs no rounds.
- **A judgement for a reason.** A round by plan tunes the libraries to the simulator's assumptions; a candidate with a reason of its own answers something that matters, and its judgement is worth its runs. D, E and F may come this way, with the reason named by `engine`.
- **Fairness first.** The finding of R58 is a risk to children before the first child from the US (dpia 10.2 spec), not a gain of a few hundredths; R58 already said that a round to bring the base within the bounds needs a target of its own.
- **No task of summing up.** Without rounds by plan an epic is short, and its last task can archive it.

— *Rejected:*
- E6 next, the proposal of T65 — a round by plan, on candidates whose rough look already fails a constraint (sources 4.9);
- E8 next — it has no candidates;
- no judgements at all — a library's candidate with a reason would have no way in but the gate, which does not choose.

— *Consequence:* the 2026-11-15 amendments to R44 and R54. Row В24 of table 3 of RUN.md, «в реестр spec», tells `spec`, `engine` and `learner-model`; row В22 records the answer (a); rows В16 and В20 point to В24. The epic E10 of table 2 is the judgement of the round of fairness. Where it is written: RUN.md, tables 1–3 and «Как устроен план: эпики»; [architecture.md](architecture.md), the status and "What stays open" of section 3; README.

— *Amendment 2026-11-16 (R64):* `learner-model` took the round as its epic E8 (sources 3.10), so E10 left the queue of table 2 and is the current epic, T69–T73; its criterion is written down by T70, before the first candidate.

## The tag `spec` v0.18.3

**R60. `sim` stands on `spec` v0.18.3, the latest tag: the submodule, `go.mod` and `gate.json` are on it with the same bits, so the paths, the bands and the runs of the base stay. The move is epic E11 of its own, «Переход на тег `spec` v0.18.3», in two tasks: T66 — the tag, the devcontainer, `.claude/` and the budgets; T67 — the layout and the language of the plan by R153–R155 spec, row Д250 spec. The plugin `gopls-lsp` loads by `CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_DIRS` of the devcontainer, not by the marketplace `novatrail` (Д248 spec); `docs-budgets.json` names the limit of R04 alone by `entries` (Д236 spec; Д9 closed). Table 3 of RUN.md is empty: `spec` recorded every row of it, В15, В16 and В18–В24.** The author's word 2026-11-16, «Сделай эпик E14» in `spec`'s orchestrator: its wave 0 moves each repository of the epic to the latest tag of `spec` first (R156 spec), and its text of the task, T66 here, asks for a split by rule 11 when the move is more than one check.
- **What the tag brings `sim`** (sources 7.9):
  - nothing the simulator reads: `spec.go`, `go.mod` of the module `spec`, `format/` and `types/formatv0` are as at v0.17.3;
  - T149 — the key `entries` of the budgets, which Д236 waited for (R51);
  - T162 — the plugin by `CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_DIRS` in `containerEnv`, and `.claude/settings.json` without `extraKnownMarketplaces` and `enabledPlugins` (the 2026-11-15 amendment to R143 spec; Д248 spec);
  - R153–R155 spec — tables 2, 3 and 4 of the plan in `run/`, the plan in English and terse for the session, and the tasks outside an epic in `run/TASKS.md` (Д250 spec);
  - R156 spec — the orchestrator: RULES.md lets «Сделай эпик E…» commit, open the pull request and merge it on green checks, so rule 7 of RUN.md names both exceptions — the author's «доведи до мержа» and the tasks of «Сделай эпик E…» — and stays «Не коммитить» for the rest;
  - the template: the base image `devcontainers/base` 3.0.13, the comments on the stages and on what the image copies; Go, gopls and its lock file are the same;
  - the card 3.9 spec with the text of option (a) of В23, and table 5 of `spec` with В15, В16 and В18–В24 at the fingerprints that RUN.md gives them;
  - Д146 and Д88 of `spec` — the cached `toolchain` of `.github` from v0.1.22 and the group `shared-workflows` of Dependabot: they do not wait for a tag of `spec`, and they are Д10 in DEBT.md. Д34 waits for the author's project on bestpractices.dev.
- **What is changed here (T66):**
  - `spec/` — v0.18.3; `go.mod` and `go.sum` — the Go module `spec` v0.18.3, the hash of its `go.mod` the same; `gate.json` — `spec` v0.18.3, the bands not written anew;
  - `.devcontainer/` — by the template: the base image, its comments and `CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_DIRS`; `sim`'s own comments stay (R11, R32); `.claude/settings.json` — the template's;
  - `docs-budgets.json` — `{"entries": {"R04": 90}}` instead of `"entry": 90`: every other entry keeps `spec`'s 40 KB;
  - CLAUDE.md, README.md, the opening of RUN.md and the status of [architecture.md](architecture.md) — the tag and the plugin; RUN.md — rule 7 with the exception of R156 spec, epic E11 in table 1, table 3 without its rows, and the paragraph of R59 that stood in table 1 between epics moved to [docs/plan/E9.md](plan/E9.md); DEBT.md — Д9 closed, Д10; [sources.md](sources.md) — record 7.9.
- **The bits are the same:** `just gate exact` on v0.18.3 — all 69 numbers to the bit; `just rough engine runs/t66-100.jsonl 100` — the 6,800 lines of the first 100 learners of each of the 68 cells byte for byte their lines in `runs/base-t64.jsonl`, the decisive run of the base whose SHA-256, `9e89cb7c…7763`, `paths.json` names, and the header differs by `"learners"` alone.

— *Why:* the author's word, and the repository stands on the latest tag of `spec` (RULES.md, Across repositories); the other tasks of E14 of `spec` — row 4.18 of its COORDINATION.md, E10 here — go on this tag. A move with the same bits touches no number of a judgement (R45). The move takes an epic of its own, because R50 puts such a task first in the next epic, and E10, the one in table 2, waits for `learner-model` to take its round (row 4.18 spec). The plugin moves now, since a marketplace on the shared `~/.claude` holds one container's path and loses the others' gopls (Д248 spec). The plan's new layout and its translation are a check of their own: they rewrite RUN.md, DEBT.md and CLAUDE.md, add four files, and T67 weighs the template's rule 9 and `run/TASKS.md` against R47 and R50, as R51 weighed the template of v0.17.3.

— *Rejected:*
- the whole move, with the plan's layout and translation, in one task — more than one check (rule 11);
- T66 as the first task of E10 — E10 waits for the round of `learner-model`, and it would stand current with nothing to do;
- the rows В15, В16 and В18–В24 kept in table 3 — RUN.md sends a row «в реестр spec» away once `spec` has recorded it, and table 5 of `spec` has recorded each as it stands;
- `"entry": 90` kept — any entry would grow to 90 KB unflagged.

— *Consequence:* the 2026-11-16 amendments to R45, R47 and R51. The merge changes `go.mod` and `gate.json` and so releases a tag of `sim` with the same numbers (R29). A rebuild of the container takes the base image and the plugin by its path — the author's step, and the orchestrator waits for it before the next task. Where it is written: RUN.md, tables 1–3; [docs/plan/E11.md](plan/E11.md), T66; CLAUDE.md; README.md; `.devcontainer/`; `.claude/settings.json`; `docs-budgets.json`; DEBT.md; [architecture.md](architecture.md), the status and "What stays open" of section 3; [sources.md](sources.md), record 7.9.

## Epic E12

**R62. Epic E12 is «Переход CI на последний релиз `.github`»: one task, T68, which carries out Д10 — the shared checks of `.github` on its latest release, with the cached `toolchain` image (Д146 spec), and the Dependabot group `shared-workflows` (Д88 spec). It is laid out now, while E10 waits for `learner-model`, and it is the current epic.** The author's answer 2026-11-16 to row В25, option (a), «своим эпиком сейчас — одна задача, как E11 для перехода на тег», given through the run «Сделай эпик» of `spec` (T171 spec) and recorded in T67.
- **The task:** T68 — the latest release of `.github`, verified, as a record of [sources.md](sources.md); the SHA of its tag in every `uses:` of `.github/workflows/`; the group in `.github/dependabot.yml`; Д10 closed; the archive of E12, its measurement and the proposal of the next (R59). Its body is RUN.md, table 1 and «Epic E12».

— *Why:* Д10's «when» is before the next CI task, and while it waits every job builds its image from the network (Д146 spec). E10 waits for `learner-model` with no date, and between epics no task runs (R50), so the debt would wait with it; an epic of its own is how R60 laid out the move to a tag, E11, for the same reason.

— *Rejected* (the options of В25): (b) the first task of E10, whenever it is laid out — the debt waits for another repository; (c) waiting for the next CI task — the same risk until then.

**R63. CI of `sim` calls the shared checks of `.github` at v0.1.24, its latest release, by the SHA of its tag, fab102d, in every `uses:` of `.github/workflows/`; its own job "Gate" takes the image of the stage `toolchain` from the cache of GitHub Actions by the steps of the shared `go.yml` — the same file, path and key, so one entry serves both —, and Dependabot raises every reference to `.github` in one pull request, the group `shared-workflows`. The tests of the root package check the mode of a written file on every build but wasip1. Д10 is closed, and epic E12 is done.** T68 of epic E12 (R62); Д146 spec and Д88 spec.
- **What v0.1.24 brings** (sources 5.3):
  - each job of `go.yml` and `documents.yml` takes the image of `toolchain` from the caller's cache, by the key `toolchain-<OS>-<architecture>-<sha256 .devcontainer/Dockerfile>`, and builds it only on a miss (R36 .github; the 2026-11-09 amendment to R31 spec); `documents.yml` does it through `TOOLCHAIN_IMAGE_FILE` of `spec`'s `toolchain.sh`, which `spec` v0.18.3 has;
  - "Go / targets" and "Go / arm64" run the tests in wasip1, in `node:wasi`, where they built and vetted it before (R39 .github);
  - no input changes; `release.yml` and `scorecard.yml` change in comments, `secrets.yml`, `dco.yml`, `codeql.yml` and the tool of the rules not at all.
- **What is changed here:**
  - `.github/workflows/` — the seven references, from `706f406 # v0.1.21` to `fab102d # v0.1.24`; in `ci.yml`, the job "Gate" — the steps «Toolchain image from the cache», «Toolchain image» and «Toolchain image into the cache» of `go.yml` v0.1.24 in place of `docker build`;
  - `.github/dependabot.yml` — the group `shared-workflows`, `patterns: ["altnovatrail/.github/*"]`, under `github-actions`, by the template of architecture 3.3 .github;
  - `gate_test.go` — `fileModes`: `TestMerge` and `TestGateWhole` check the mode of a file of results and of the gate file only where `GOOS` is not `wasip1`. WASI preview1 has no modes, and Go's `Fchmod` there only checks that the file is there (sources 5.3), so in `node:wasi` a new file had the mode the host gave it, `-rw-------`, and `TestMerge` failed — the one test of all that did. `TestGateWhole` passed there only because the mode it wants, 0600, is the one the host gives, not because the record kept it, so its check is held off there too. `writeTo` stays: the simulator is not built for wasip1 (section 3.9 of [architecture.md](architecture.md));
  - the comment of the recipe `test-targets`, which runs the script of the pinned `targets`, and wasip1 with it; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3 and 3.9; [sources.md](sources.md), record 5.3.

— *Why:* Д10 — while CI is on v0.1.21, each job builds the image from the network, and an outage of nodejs.org, GitHub or the snapshot of the Ubuntu archive fails the checks (Д146 spec); "Gate" is one such job, twice, so it takes the cache too, as the task's check asks. One entry for "Gate" and the jobs of `go.yml`: the same Dockerfile, the same command and the same image `toolchain`, and an entry of an image is about 575 MB of the 10 GB of a repository (the 2026-11-09 amendment to R31 spec). One pull request for all the references to `.github`, since they name one release (Д88 spec). The test, not the code: a mode wasip1 cannot set is no fault of the simulator, which runs on Linux.

— *Rejected:*
- "Gate" building its image on every run — the risk of Д10 stays for the gate;
- a path or key of the gate's own — a second entry of the same image in the cache. The copy of the steps has its price: Dependabot moves the SHA of `go.yml`, not the copy, so a pull request that moves `.github` holds the steps to its `go.yml` by hand, as [.claude/rules/ci.md](../.claude/rules/ci.md) says; `.github` has no shared action for the steps alone;
- `t.Skip` of `TestMerge` on wasip1 — the merge to the byte goes unchecked there too;
- a file of its own for wasip1 by a build tag — the rule `buildtag` of the rules of code;
- waiting for a later release of `.github` — v0.1.24 has what Д10 needs, and a later one comes by Dependabot, now in one pull request.

— *Consequence:* the 2026-11-16 amendments to R10, R29 and R62. The merge changes the justfile and `release.yml`, which the release is built from, and so releases a tag of `sim` with the same numbers (R29). Epic E12 is in its archive, [docs/plan/E12.md](plan/E12.md), with the measurement of its sessions in measurements 2.2; table 1 has no current epic, and the next is the author's choice: E10 of `run/EPICS.md` waits for `learner-model` to take its round (row 4.18 of COORDINATION.md spec), and between epics no task runs (R50, R59). Where it is written: `.github/workflows/`; `.github/dependabot.yml`; `gate_test.go`; the justfile; RUN.md, table 1; [docs/plan/E12.md](plan/E12.md); `run/DEBT.md`; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3 and 3.9 and "What stays open"; [sources.md](sources.md), record 5.3; [measurements.md](measurements.md), section 2.2; README.md.

## Epic E10

**R64. Epic E10 is «Суд круга справедливости модели», the judgement of the round of fairness by the grade that `learner-model` took as its epic E8. It is the current epic, in five tasks: T69 lays it out; T70 writes down its criterion before the first candidate — the model's criterion as is, the target `M11.same` of `D-grade1` and `D-grade5`, a folder and a held-out set of its own —; T71 builds the target and the set in `sim judge`, and its merge releases the tag the judgement pins; T72 judges the candidates of `learner-model` on the commits of their draft pull requests, confirms the chosen one or records the exit, and sends the outcome «в реестр spec» for dpia 10.2 spec; T73 moves the base to the release of the chosen one, with the evaluation `fairness-2`, and archives the epic.** T69, written by the text that `spec`'s orchestrator gave for row 4.18 of its COORDINATION.md in the run «Сделай эпик E…» (R156 spec; rule 6 of COORDINATION.md spec); the split into tasks is the executor's, since in a run of the orchestrator nobody approves a plan, and its questions are rows of table 3 (R156 spec).
- **What was found** (2026-11-16, sources 3.10): `learner-model` laid out E8 at `main` e3aaee6 (R40 learner-model), no task of it done. Its T60 moves "Gate" to the tag of `sim` on which the criterion of E10 is written down, its exploration T61 reads its sets by `sim judge` with that criterion, its candidates are draft pull requests, T62, and its T63 waits for the judgement of E10 on the candidate's commit, not for a release of `sim`. Its latest release, `v0.1.10`, changes no Go file and no `go.mod` against `v0.1.8`, the base. The latest tag of `sim` is `v0.1.22`. В22 and В24 are no longer in table 3 — `spec` v0.18.3 recorded them (R60) —, and no row of tables 3 and 4 names E10.
- **The tasks:**
  - T70 — the criterion, «Claude, проверяю я», with planning: how the target reads `M11.same` of the two grades and what "better" is, whether the bound 0.90 of R55 is a condition of the choice or only the outcome for dpia 10.2 spec, the constraints as they are, the names `model-fair-1` and `held-out-model-fair-1` by the rule of R49 and the seed as the date of writing (section 3.8 of [architecture.md](architecture.md)); it waits for nothing in `learner-model`;
  - T71 — the code and the tag: `M11` is in no score group and has no path and no target (R55), so `sim judge` cannot score the target now, and the set is a row of `internal/seed/sets.go` (R52). It moves no bit of the runs, so the base's file of 68 cells and the 69 numbers of the gate stay; it is the first tag with the criterion, since T70 changes only documents and releases none (R29), and so the tag that T60 and T61 of `learner-model` wait for; the judgement pins it, later than `v0.1.20` and the latest `v0.1.22`, with `fair` and that file (the 2026-11-14 amendment to R57);
  - T72 — the judgement `model-fair-1`, `CANDIDATE_LEARNER_MODEL=<sha> just decisive engine` on each candidate's commit (R34), the confirmation once on its own set, the bands of the chosen one and the tag that `learner-model`'s release waits for (R103 spec), or the exit; it waits for the draft pull requests of T62 `learner-model`, not their release;
  - T73 — «я + Claude», with planning: the base on the release of the chosen one, the paths and the evaluation `fairness-2` anew (R55), its decisive run on a free machine, or, after an exit, the archive alone, the measurement of its sessions and the proposal of the next epic (R59).

— *Why:* R59 queued this judgement for the moment `learner-model` takes the round, and E8 is that moment. The criterion goes first and apart from its code, as T61 did in E9: the author reads it while nothing runs it. The judgement goes on the commits, since a release of the chosen one waits for the bands of `sim` (R103 spec). A separate evaluation after the move of the base, since R55 repeats the evaluation whenever the base moves.

— *Rejected:*
- the criterion and its code in one task — the author would read the criterion beside the code, and one review would not hold both, as in E9;
- the judgement waiting for the release of the candidates — the release waits for the judgement's bands (R103 spec), a cycle;
- a task of summing up of its own — R59: the last task archives the epic;
- the evaluation of the chosen one taken from the judgement's report alone — R55 evaluates the base, by `sim evaluate`, whenever it moves.

— *Consequence:* the 2026-11-16 amendments to R57, R59, R61 and R63; table 2 is empty. Where it is written: RUN.md, table 1 and «Epic E10»; [docs/plan/E10.md](plan/E10.md), T69; `run/EPICS.md`; [architecture.md](architecture.md), "What stays open"; [sources.md](sources.md), record 3.10; README.md.

## The judgement of the round of fairness

**R65. The judgement of the round of fairness by the grade, that of epic E8 of `learner-model`, goes by the model's criterion of section 3.6 as it is, and its target is `M11.same` of `D-grade1` and `D-grade5` pooled: the mean of the two paired differences, the candidate's less the base's, with one 95 % interval, its lower end above zero. The target alone decides "better" and the choice; the model's score stands in the report as a reference. The bounds of R55 decide nothing here: the report says whether the chosen one holds them on its confirmation, and the outcome goes «в реестр spec» for dpia 10.2 spec. The judgement is `judgements/model-fair-1/`, and its held-out set `held-out-model-fair-1`, seed 20261116. Written down before the first candidate.** The author's answers 2026-11-16 to rows В26–В28 of table 3, all by option (a), given through the run «Сделай эпик E…» of `spec`'s orchestrator (R156 spec); the rest is the executor's, by the rules of R49 and of section 3.8 of [architecture.md](architecture.md) (RUN.md, T70). Row 4.18 of `spec`'s COORDINATION.md asks for it: «критерий модели как есть, цель — `M11.same` у `D-grade1` и `D-grade5`», option (a) of В22 (R59).
- **The constraints** are those of the model's criterion as it is: `I1`–`I4`; the floors of `M4` and `M6` on `G0` and `G0-skills1`; "no worse" than the base on every population of the 65 cells with a verdict and on their own populations; and `fair` on `D-grade1` and `D-grade5` — the shortfall of `M11.same`, `M11.answers` and `M11.band`, each "no worse" than the base (R55, R57). `fair` does not change because `M11.same` is the target: it keeps each grade from getting worse, so a pooled gain cannot come from one grade's loss beyond the tolerance. The cells `D-` are still read by the target and `fair` alone (the 2026-11-14 amendment to R55).
- **The target.** For grade g, Δg is `M11.same` of the candidate less that of the base — the share as it is, more being better, so Δg is the base's shortfall less the candidate's, the opposite sign of the difference `fair` holds —, each read off the pairs of the grade's learner n and the learner n of `D-grade3` on the same side, as `fair` and `sim evaluate` read them (R56, R57). The target's score is (Δ1 + Δ5) / 2, a share of learners, with no path: `M11` has no ceiling, since the oracle's level is one under every grade (R56). Its 95 % interval is from the same 2,000 resamples of n, the same n in both grades and both sides, by the reference's cell (R57).
- **"Better" and the choice.** A candidate is better than the base when the lower end of the interval of its target's score is above zero, and better than another candidate when the interval of the paired difference of their target's scores is above zero. Among the candidates that hold all the constraints and are better than the base, A* is the one with the largest target's score; its equals and the simplest of them are by section 3.6. A candidate names no score group. The model's score — the score groups of section 3.6 on the 65 cells, and each group's, with their intervals — is written beside each candidate and decides nothing.
- **The confirmation** — once, by a decisive run on `held-out-model-fair-1`, beside the base on the same set: the chosen one holds all the constraints and the lower end of the interval of its target's score is above zero there too, as at the choice; if not, the exit, and the base stays (section 3.6).
- **The bounds of R55** — `M11.same` at least 0.90 at the lower end and `M11.band` at most 0.05 at the upper end, for grades 1 and 5 alike — are no condition of the choice or of the confirmation. The report writes, for the confirmation of the chosen one, whether its run holds them. The outcome — `M11` of the chosen one on both grades with the intervals of the confirmation and whether it holds the bounds, or the exit with the numbers of the judgement — goes «в реестр spec» for row 4.18 and item 10 of dpia 10.2 spec (T72), and the base moved to the chosen one's release is evaluated anew, `evaluations/fairness-2/` (T73; R55).
- **The report** writes, for each candidate and for the confirmation: `M11.answers`, `M11.same` and `M11.band` of the candidate and of the base on both grades, with their intervals; Δ1, Δ5 and the target's score with their intervals; the model's score as a reference; and, for the confirmation, the bounds of R55.
- **The names.** A judgement of one variant apart from the rounds of its criterion has a folder and a set of its own, `<criterion>-<variant>-<n>` (R49): `judgements/model-fair-1/` and `held-out-model-fair-1`. The round of the model's criterion stays `model-3`, with `held-out-model-2`, when a candidate with a reason of its own brings it (R59). The seed is 20261116, the date of writing, by the rule of section 3.8; the 2026-11-16 amendment to R04 records it.
- **What T71 builds.** `sim judge` reads no target off `M11` now: `M11` is in no score group and has no path or target (R55), and the candidate's group is a score group. T71 builds the target off the pairs `fair` already reads, its report and the line `model-fair-1` — `held-out-model-fair-1` of the table of `internal/seed/sets.go` with its seed (R52); how the judgement file names the target is its planning. It moves no bit of a run: the base's file of 68 cells that `paths.json` names, the paths and the 69 numbers of the gate stay. Its merge releases the tag the judgement pins (R29; the 2026-11-16 amendment to R57).

— *Why:*
- **The model's criterion as it is.** The round should make the model fairer without making it worse elsewhere, and the criterion already holds every cell "no worse" (row 4.18 spec; R59).
- **A pooled share.** A candidate that mends the start above the truth — the question of T59 `learner-model` (sources 3.10) — may lift grade 5 alone, and `fair` keeps grade 1 from getting worse. The number reads for dpia 10.2 spec as a share of the learners of the two grades, each counting alike.
- **The target alone.** "No worse" on the 65 cells already holds what the score reads, and a candidate of fairness need not gain anything there: the rough look with σ₀ 3.0 of T30 `learner-model` was no better than the base by the score (sources 3.10). The round's aim is fairness, a risk to children (R59).
- **The bounds reported.** A fairer base serves children better than the base as it is, whether or not it reaches the bound; the bound is the evaluation's, and the evaluation records it (R55, R58).
- **A set of its own.** A set one judgement has looked at is held out no longer, and the round `model-3` keeps `held-out-model-2` (R49, R52).

— *Rejected:*
- (b) of В26, each grade apart, both lower ends above zero — a candidate that lifts grade 5 alone is not chosen; (c), a score group, `D-grade1` 0.5 and `D-grade5` 0.5, as a share of the path to perfection — a point of grade 1, whose path is 0.188 against 0.4475, would weigh 2.4 times a point of grade 5;
- (b) of В27, the model's score not wholly below zero, as for a pair's second library — a condition no constraint needs; (c), the score above zero too, as R49 — a fair candidate with no gain elsewhere would never be chosen;
- (b) of В28, the bounds as a condition of the choice and of the confirmation — a candidate that brought grade 5 from 0.55 to 0.85 would be an exit, and the base would stay less fair;
- `M11.same` in a score group of the model's criterion for every judgement — it would change the criterion of `model-3` and of any other round, and row 4.18 asks for the criterion as it is;
- `fair` left off `M11.same` because it is the target — one grade could then lose what the other gains;
- `M11.answers` in the target — row 4.18 names `M11.same`, and `fair` holds `M11.answers`.

— *Consequence:* the 2026-11-16 amendments to R04, R52, R55 and R64; rows В26–В28 left table 3. T71 builds the target and the set and releases the tag; T72 judges by this entry. Where it is written: RUN.md, T70 ([docs/plan/E10.md](plan/E10.md)); [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.6 and 3.8 and "What stays open" of section 3; README.

— *Amendment 2026-11-16 (T71; R66):* built. The judgement file of `model-fair-1` names `"target": "M11.same"`, which `sim judge` requires of it and refuses of every other judgement; its candidates name no group or goal. The target is read off the pairs of `fair` with `M11` as it is, and the report writes what this entry asks; the row of its set is in `internal/seed/sets.go`. The tag its merge releases is named by T72, by an amendment to this entry.
